  DateTime @updatedAt\n\n  donations Donation[]\n}\n\nmodel Donation {\n  id        String   @id @default(cuid())\n  alumnusId String\n  amount    Float\n  method    String   @default(\"M-Pesa\") // @default(now())\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Inventory Requests (staff request items from store/kitchen)\n// String?\n  quantity        Int       @default(1)\n  unit            String    @default(\"pcs\") // \"kg\" | \"litres\" | \"pcs\" | \"boxes\" | \"packs\"\n  urgency         String    @default(\"Normal\") // \"Low\" | \"Normal\" | \"High\" | \"Urgent\"\n  requestedBy     String\n  requesterRole   String    @default(\"Staff\")\n  department      String? // \"Kitchen\" | \"Library\"       DateTime  @default(now())\n  updatedAt       DateTime  @updatedAt\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Activity log (audit trail)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodel ActivityLog {\n  id        String   @id @default(cuid())\n  action    String\n  entity    String\n  entityId  String?\n  user      String?\n  details   String?\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now())\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// BIOMETRIC ATTENDANCE SYSTEM\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tracks biometric devices (ZKTeco K40, USB scanners, bus tablets), enrolled\n// fingerprint/face templates, and the live tap log. Designed to work with or\n// without real hardware — the UI includes a built-in simulator for demos.\n\nmodel BiometricDevice {\n  id         String    @id @default(cuid())\n  schoolId   String\n  name       String // e.g. \"Main Gate Scanner\", \"Bus 3 Tablet\"\n  deviceType String    @default(\"fingerprint\") // \"fingerprint\" | \"rfid\" | \"face\" | \"tablet\"\n  location   String? // e.g. \"Main Gate\", \"Bus 3\"\n  vehicleId  String? // if deviceType is \"tablet\" — which bus it's mounted in\n  status     String    @default(\"active\") // \"active\" | \"inactive\" | \"offline\"\n  lastSeen   DateTime?\n  secret     String    @default(cuid()) // shared secret for HMAC auth on /sync\n  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now())\n  updatedAt  DateTime  @updatedAt\n\n  logs BiometricLog[]\n}\n\nmodel BiometricTemplate {\n  id           String   @id @default(cuid())\n  schoolId     String\n  personId     String // Student.id or Staff.id\n  personType   String   @default(\"student\") // \"student\" | \"staff\"\n  templateHash String   @unique // one-way hash of the biometric template (never store raw)\n  fingerIndex  Int      @default(0) // 0 = thumb, 1 = index, etc.\n  isActive     Boolean  @default(true)\n  enrolledAt   DateTime @default(now())\n  enrolledBy   String? // UserAccount.id of the admin who enrolled\n  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())\n}\n\nmodel BiometricLog {\n  id         String           @id @default(cuid())\n  schoolId   String\n  deviceId   String?\n  device     BiometricDevice? @relation(fields: [deviceId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)\n  personId   String // Student.id or Staff.id\n  personType String           @default(\"student\") // \"student\" | \"staff\" | \"unknown\"\n  action     String // \"check_in\" | \"check_out\" | \"board_bus\" | \"alight_bus\"\n  location   String? // human-readable location\n  gps        String? // \"lat,lng\" if device has GPS\n  verified   Boolean          @default(true) // false if the template didn't match\n  timestamp  DateTime         @default(now())\n\n  createdAt DateTime @default(now())\n\n  @@index([schoolId, timestamp])\n  @@index([personId, timestamp])\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// BUS TRACKING SYSTEM\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodel BusTrip {\n  id            String    @id @default(cuid())\n  schoolId      String\n  routeId       String?\n  vehicleId     String?\n  driverId      String?\n  direction     String    @default(\"to_school\") // \"to_school\" | \"from_school\"\n  status        String    @default(\"scheduled\") // \"scheduled\" | \"in_progress\" | \"completed\" | \"cancelled\"\n  departureAt   DateTime?\n  arrivalAt     DateTime?\n  boardingCount Int       @default(0) // denormalized for fast dashboard queries\n  gpsTrail      String?
